```
#
# EMPTY AT THE TOP LEVEL IS HOST-ONLY AND IS CORRECT FOR `wrangler dev`, which
# serves on `127.0.0.1` — nobody's cookie domain. A NAME is still set there,
# because an empty name means this deployment issues no sessions at all and the
# local builder would have no way to sign anybody in.
[vars]

# [[BUG-359]] — the job container's `instance_type` (the `[[containers]]` entry
# below), restated where the Worker can read it: the job's memory limit is looked
# up from it (`INSTANCE_MEMORY_MB`). A UAT holds every environment's copy equal
# to its `[[containers]]` entry, so the two cannot drift.

SERVICE_TOKEN_IDENTITIES = ""
ACCESS_DEV_OPEN = "1"
ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN = "lagrangefoundry.cloudflareaccess.com"
```

```
  async runStep(request: StepRequest): Promise<ContainerStepResult> {
    const started = this.now()
    await this.clock({ inflightSince: started, idleSince: started }, started + INFLIGHT_STOP_MS)
    try {
      return await this.step(request, started)
    } finally {
      if (request.kind === 'end') await this.end()
      else {
        const done = this.now()
        await this.clock({ inflightSince: null, idleSince: done }, done + IDLE_STOP_MS)
      }
    }
  }

  /** Record the step clock and arm the alarm for when it would next stop the container. */

    await this.storage.setAlarm(at)
```

```json
{"command":"cat > tests/test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_dev_step_over_instance_limit_fails_as_oom.test.ts <<'EOF'\n/**\n * [[BUG-359]] fix 4 — ON DEV AND THE UAT RIG, A STEP OVER ITS INSTANCE TYPE'S LIMIT FAILS AS `oom`.\n *\n * Local containers are created by workerd with no memory limit, so a job that\n * production's `standard-1` (4 GiB) would kill finished on dev. The job is now told\n * its instance type's limit — looked up from `instance_type`, never hard-coded —\n * and, where `JOB_MEMORY_ENFORCE=1` (dev, the container suites), a step whose\n * whole-container peak goes past it is answered as a memory kill and the container\n * exits 137, as production's kill would. The Workflow records `exit_reason=oom`\n * and counts it in `memory_kills`. Without the flag (production) the peak and the\n * limit are only recorded.\n *\n * Three layers, each real where it can be:\n *  - the probe tripping, in a Linux container as the job server runs, against the\n *    `lite` instance type's limit;\n *  - the job server answering the trip, through `JobContainer` and the turn's row\n *    in a `turn_log` built from the real migrations;\n *  - the configuration: every environment's `JOB_INSTANCE_TYPE` is its\n *    `[[containers]]` `instance_type`, and only dev enforces.\n */\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'\nimport path from 'node:path'\nimport { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite'\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'\nimport { experimental_readRawConfig } from 'wrangler'\nimport { startJobServer } from '../apps/control-app/container/server'\nimport { containerEnv, memoryLimitMb } from '../apps/control-app/src/agent-container'\nimport { turnRow, type TurnLogEnv } from '../apps/control-app/src/turn-log'\nimport { jobUnderTest, roundRequest } from './support/fake-job-container'\nimport { inLinuxContainer } from './support/linux-memory'\n\nconst REPO = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..')\nconst cleanups: (() => Promise<void> | void)[] = []\nafterEach(async () => {\n  for (const c of cleanups.splice(0).reverse()) await c()\n})\n\n/** A `turn_log` from the migrations that shape it, behind the D1 calls `turn-log.ts` makes. */\nfunction turnLog(): { env: TurnLogEnv; row(id: string): Record<string, unknown> } {\n  const db = new DatabaseSync(':memory:')\n  for (const file of ['0021_turn_log.sql', '0033_turn_log_alive.sql', '0048_turn_log_compute.sql', '0050_turn_log_memory_source.sql']) {\n    const sql = readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'db', 'migrations', file), 'utf8').replace(/--.*$/gm, '')\n    for (const statement of sql.split(';').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)) db.exec(statement)\n  }\n  const prepare = (sql: string) => {\n    let args: unknown[] = []\n    const stmt = {\n      bind: (...a: unknown[]) => ((args = a), stmt),\n      run: async () => db.prepare(sql).run(...(args as never[])),\n    }\n    return stmt\n  }\n  return {\n    env: { DB: { prepare } } as unknown as TurnLogEnv,\n    row: (id) => db.prepare('SELECT * FROM turn_log WHERE turn_id = ?').get(id) as Record<string, unknown>,\n  }\n}\n\n/** A `JobContainer` whose container is the real job server, started with what the DO would start it with. */\nasync function jobOnRealServer(env: Record<string, unknown>, readings: number[]) {\n  const t = jobUnderTest(env)\n  const exits: number[] = []\n  let server: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof startJobServer>> | null = null\n  const start = t.container.start.bind(t.container)\n  t.container.start = (options) => {\n    start(options)\n    server = null\n    void startJobServer({\n      vars: { ...options.env, PLATFORM_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:9' },\n      exit: (code) => {\n        exits.push(code)\n        void t.container.destroy()\n      },\n      port: 0,\n      memory: { source: 'cgroup', read: async () => (readings.length > 1 ? readings.shift()! : readings[0]!) },\n      sampleEveryMs: 20,\n      execute: () => new Promise(() => {}),\n    }).then((s) => {\n      server = s\n      cleanups.push(() => new Promise<void>((r) => s.server.close(() => r())))\n    })\n  }\n  t.container.getTcpPort = () => ({\n    fetch: async (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => {\n      while (!server) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5))\n      return fetch(input.replace(/^http:\\/\\/container/, `http://127.0.0.1:${server.port}`), init)\n    },\n  })\n  return { t, exits }\n}\n\ndescribe('BUG-359 — a dev step over its instance limit fails as oom', () => {\n  it('test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_dev_step_over_instance_limit_fails_as_oom', async () => {\n    // THE LIMIT IS THE INSTANCE TYPE'S, and the container is told it with the flag.\n    const env = { JOB_INSTANCE_TYPE: 'lite', JOB_MEMORY_ENFORCE: '1' }\n    const limit = memoryLimitMb(env)!\n    expect(limit).toBe(256)\n    const started = containerEnv(env, 'job-bug359', 'token')\n    expect(started).toMatchObject({ JOB_MEMORY_LIMIT_MB: '256', JOB_MEMORY_ENFORCE: '1' })\n\n    // A STEP WHOSE CHILD GOES PAST IT TRIPS, measured in Linux as the job server measures it.\n    const seen = inLinuxContainer(400, limit)\n    expect(seen.chosen.over).toBeGreaterThan(limit)\n\n    // THE SERVER ENDS THE STEP AS A MEMORY KILL, and the turn's row says so.\n    const { t, exits } = await jobOnRealServer(env, [120, 180, seen.chosen.over!])\n    const log = turnLog()\n    await log.env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO turn_log (turn_id, tenant_id, t.job.runStep(roundRequest(0))\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(false)\n    expect(result.compute).toMatchObject({ exitReason: 'oom', peakMemorySource: 'cgroup', memoryLimitMb: 256 })\n    expect(exits).toEqual([137])\n    expect(t.container.running).toBe(false)\n
```

```
  async alarm(): Promise<void> {
    const clock = await this.storage.get<StepClock>('steps')
    if (!clock) return
    const now = this.now()
    const due = clock.inflightSince !== null ? clock.inflightSince + INFLIGHT_STOP_MS : clock.idleSince + IDLE_STOP_MS
    if (now < due) return this.storage.setAlarm(due)

  }

  private async step(request: StepRequest, started: number): Promise<ContainerStepResult> {
    const compute = (reason: ExitReason, server: ServerCompute | null = null): StepCompute => ({
      peakMemoryMb: server?.peakMemoryMb ?? null,
      cpuS: server?.cpuS ?? null,
      wallS: (this.now() - started) / 1000,
      exitReason: reason,
      peakMemorySource: server?.peakMemorySource ?? null,
      memoryLimitMb: server?.memoryLimitMb ?? null,
    })
    let response: Response
    try {
      await this.ready(request.job.id)
      // RACED AGAINST THE CONTAINER'S OWN END. A request to a container that was
      // killed is not guaranteed to fail on its own; the container's exit, as the
      // platform reports it or as `running` turns false, is what ends the step.
      const stopped = this.stopped()
      try {
        response = await Promise.race([
          this.container.getTcpPort(CONTAINER_PORT).fetch('http://container/step', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(request),
          }),
          stopped.promise,
        ])
      } finally {
        stopped.cancel()
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // THE CONTAINER STOPPED ANSWERING: ask the platform how it ended.
      const exit = await Promise.race([
        this.exited ?? Promise.resolve<ContainerExit>({}),
        new Promise<ContainerExit>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve({}), EXIT_WITHIN_MS)),
      ])
      this.exited = null
      const why = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      return { ok: false, message: `the job's container stopped: ${why}`, compute: compute(exitReason(exit)) }
    }
    const body = (await response.json().catch(() => null)) as
      | { answer?: StepAnswer; compute?: ServerCompute; checkpoint?: boolean; oom?: boolean; error?: string }
      | null

      // [[BUG-359]] — THE STEP WENT OVER ITS INSTANCE TYPE'S MEMORY (dev and the

      // production's kill would end it, and the retry starts a fresh one.
      if (this.container.running) await this.container.destroy().catch(() => {})
      this.exited = null
      return { ok: false, message: body.error ?? 'the job\'s container went over its memory limit', compute: compute('oom', body.compute ?? null) }
    }
    if (response.ok && body?.answer) {
      return { ok: true, answer: body.answer, compute: compute('ok', body.compute ?? null) }
    }
    if (response.status === 409) {
      // AN EARLIER ATTEMPT IS STILL RUNNING in a container the Workflow already
      // gave up on. It is stopped, so the retry starts in a fresh one rather than
      // racing it on the junction.
      if (this.container.running) await this.container.destroy().catch(() => {})
      this.exited = null
      return { ok: false, message: 'an earlier attempt was still running; its container was stopped', compute: compute('crash') }
    }
    if (body?.checkpoint) {
      return { ok: false, message: 'the job\'s container was stopped; the step checkpointed', compute: compute('sigterm', body.compute ?? null) }
    }
    return { ok: false, message: body?.error ?? `the job's container answered ${response.status}`, compute: compute('error', body?.compute ?? null) }
  }
```

```
+export function rigContainers(since: number): string[] {

+export async function removeRigContainers(since: number): Promise<string[]> {
+  for (let pass = 0; pass < 10; pass++) {

+    docker(['rm', '-f', ...left], { allowFail: true })
+    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 500))
```

```
`tests/test_UAT_FC_REQ-646_agent_compute_in_containers.test.ts` runs the control app under `unstable_dev` with real job containers (Docker).
- **A worktree needs `apps/control-app/dist-assets`**, or `unstable_dev` refuses the config. Symlink the main checkout's copy, and never stage the symlink.
- **Re-copy `apps/control-app/src/generated` after merging xgd-working into a branch.** The stale copy made BUG-322's stage-guide UAT fail on the branch only, which looked like a regression.
- **Run one case per Bash call** (`-t <name>`): each takes 1–8 min (the 400-product/2,000-item case is about 500 s), so the whole file overruns 590 s.
- `getPlatformProxy` on the full `wrangler.toml` fails because the AI binding is `remote = true`. The rig seeds through a D1/R2-only config whose persist path is `<persistTo>/v3`.

Related: [[docker-builds-need-temp-docker-config]], [[worktree-and-store-setup]], [[full-suite-in-foreground-batches]].
```

```
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process'
import path from 'node:path'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { backendsDocument } from '../tools/generate/src/cli/ai/backends'
import { ticketStoreFor } from '../apps/control-app/src/tickets'
import { classify, labelOfMaterial } from '../apps/control-app/src/material'
import { createItem, readItemInput } from '../apps/control-app/src/shop'
import { BUILDER_ROLE } from '../tools/generate/src/cli/ai/roles'
import { calls, says, sentText, type ModelRequest, type ModelStep } from './support/scripted-model-client'
import { modelServer, type ModelServer } from './support/model-server'
import {
  REPO,
  docker,
  dockerRunning,
  frames,
  jobContainers,
  sectionText,
  sectionedSite,
  startJobRig,
  type Frame,
  type JobRig,
} from './support/job-container-rig'

/**
 * [[REQ-646]] — A CONSULTANT JOB'S WORK RUNS IN ITS OWN CONTAINER ([[DOC-86]] §3).
 *
 * Every case drives the control app as `wrangler dev` runs it for local
 * development: the real Worker, the real `ConsultantTurn` Workflow, the real
 * `AgentContainer` Durable Object and the job's real container, built from
 * `apps/control-app/container/Dockerfile` and run by Docker. The client posts a
 * message and reads the stream, exactly as the builder's chat does. The model is
 * the one double, served over HTTP to the container (`model-server.ts`).
 *
 * The container is killed with `docker kill` (a crash, or a memory kill) and
 * stopped with `docker stop` (SIGTERM, a deploy) — what the platform does to a
 * container, done to the real one.
 */


const WORKER_MODEL = backendsDocument.claude_builder.model
const DONE_LINE = 'The hero is rebuilt.'

const brief = (section: string) => ({
  note: `Rebuilding the ${section}.`,
  role: BUILDER_ROLE,
  goal: `Rewrite the ${section} section.`,
  accept: [],
  scope: [`home.json#${section}`],
})

const write = (section: string, text: string): ModelStep =>
  calls('set_l1', { page: 'home', section, path: '0', node: { kind: 'text', id: section, text, axes: { fontSizePx: 16 } } })

const report = (summary: string): ModelStep => calls('ReportResult', { summary, changed: [], decisions: [], passed: [], failed: [] })

const spoken = (seen: Frame[]): string => seen.filter((f) => f.kind === 'text').map((f) => f.content).join('')

/** Who the model is asked as, and the script each answers from. */
interface Script {
  consultant: ModelStep[]
  builder: (req: ModelRequest, n: number) => Promise<ModelStep | null> | ModelStep | null
}

let script: Script = { consultant: [says('Hello.')], builder: () => report('Nothing.') }
const asked = { consultant: [] as ModelRequest[], builder: [] as ModelRequest[] }

function play(next: Script): void {
  script = next
  asked.consultant.length = 0
  asked.builder.length = 0
}

const until = async (ok: () => Promise<boolean> | boolean, ms = 120_000): Promise<void> => {
  const end = Date.now() + ms
  while (!(await ok())) {
    if (Date.now() > end) throw new Error('timed out waiting')
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250))
  }
}

/** Worker secrets that must never reach a container (the Worker has them for its own calls). */
const SECRETS = {
  CLOUDFLARE_DNS_TOKEN: 'dns-token-must-not-travel',
  RESEND_API_KEY: 'resend-key-must-not-travel',
  DEVELOPMENT_TICKETS_TOKEN: 'tickets-token-must-not-travel',
  STRIPE_SECRET_KEY_TEST: 'sk_test_must_not_travel',
}

let model: ModelServer
let rig: JobRig
const DOCKER = dockerRunning()

beforeAll(async () => {
  if (!DOCKER) return
  execFileSync('node', [path.join(REPO, 'apps', 'control-app', 'container', 'build.mjs')], { stdio: 'ignore' })
  model = await modelServer(async (req) => {
    if (sentText(req).includes('You are the talker.')) return says('Still building.')
    if (req.model === WORKER_MODEL) {
      const n = asked.builder.length
      asked.builder.push(req)
      return script.builder(req, n)
    }
    const n = asked.consultant.length
    asked.consultant.push(req)
    return script.consultant[Math.min(n, script.consultant.length - 1)]!
  })
  rig = await startJobRig({ modelUrl: model.url, vars: SECRETS })
}, 900_000)

afterAll(async () => {
  await rig?.stop()
  await model?.close()
})

/** The turn's ledger row: what its containers spent, and how its steps ended. */
async function ledger(sessionId: string): Promise<Array<Record<string, unknown>>> {
  return rig.query('SELECT outcome, peak_memory_mb, cpu_s, wall_s, exit_reason, memory_kills FROM turn_log WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY started_at', sessionId)
}

describe('REQ-646 — agent compute runs in per-job containers', () => {
  it.runIf(DOCKER)('test_UAT_FC_REQ-646_full_message_runs_through_a_container', async () => {
    play({
      consultant: [calls('Delegate', brief('hero')), says(DONE_LINE)],
      builder: (_req, n) => (n === 0 ? write('hero', 'Builder hero') : report('Built the hero.')),
    })
    const { site, sessionId } = await sectionedSite(rig, 'full')

    const seen = await frames(await rig.post('/api/ai/prompt', { sessionId, text: 'Rebuild the hero.' }))

    // A CONSULTANT ROUND, A BUILDER PASS AND THE DELIVERY ROUND, each asked of the
    // model by the job's container — the Worker never runs a step. The round after
    // the pass opened with its result.
    expect(asked.consultant.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2)
    expect(JSON.stringify(asked.consultant[1]!.messages.at(-1))).toContain('The result of your Delegate call')
    expect(asked.builder.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2)
    expect(await sectionText(rig, site, 'hero')).toBe('Builder hero')
    // THE PAGE WAS LOOKED AT BY THE CONTAINER'S OWN CHROMIUM: the host measured the
    // page the pass wrote and told the consultant what it found.
    expect(asked.consultant.so
```

```
  it('test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_a_failed_registry_delete_warns_and_never_throws', async () => {
    const cf = cloudflare({ application, repository: application, deployed: 'v4', tags: ['v1', 'v2', 'v3', 'v4'], refuse: ['v1'] })
    const [result] = await pruneRegistryImages({ token: 'token', worker, classes, env: { CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ACCOUNT }, fetch: cf.fetch })
    expect(result!.deleted).toEqual(['v2'])
    expect(result!.failed.map((f) => f.tag)).toEqual(['v1'])

    // AN APPLICATION THE API CANNOT FIND IS SKIPPED, with why: nothing is guessed.
    const none = await pruneRegistryImages({ token: 'token', worker: 'some-other-worker', classes, env: { CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ACCOUNT }, fetch: cf.fetch })
    expect(none[0]!.skipped).toContain('no container application')
    expect(none[0]!.deleted).toEqual([])
  })

  it('test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_the_prune_runs_after_a_cloud_deploy_and_never_in_a_rehearsal', () => {
    const deploy = readFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'bin', 'deploy'), 'utf8')
    // AFTER THE SHIP LOOP, so only a deploy that shipped prunes; never fatal.
    const shipped = deploy.indexOf('deployed+=("$name")')
    const prune = deploy.indexOf('step "Prune old job images from the registry"')
    expect(shipped).toBeGreaterThan(-1)
    expect(prune).toBeGreaterThan(shipped)
    const block = deploy.slice(deploy.lastIndexOf('if [[', prune), deploy.indexOf('\nfi\n', prune))
    expect(block).toContain('$dry_run -eq 0')
    expect(block).toContain('"$target" != "local"')
    expect(block).toMatch(/\$prune_cmd[^\n]*\|\| echo/)

    // A REHEARSAL OF A PRODUCTION DEPLOY RUNS NO PRUNE.
    const out = execFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'bin', 'deploy'), ['--env', 'production', '--dry-run', 'public-site'], {
      cwd: REPO,
      encoding: 'utf8',
      env: { ...process.env, DEPLOY_PRUNE_IMAGES_CMD: 'echo bug359-prune-ran' },
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
      timeout: 5 * 60_000,
    })
    expect(out).not.toContain('bug359-prune-ran')
  }, 300_000)
})
```

## Bash cd ~/.xgd/worktrees/git_github.com_lagrangefoundry_1stcontact.git/free-BUG-359 && sed -n 1,160p tests/test_UAT_FC_BUG-35…
<!-- xgd-tool name="Bash" id="toolu_01J3od2dQHfmuTz31fT4mYu7" turn="78b736464f964ade910a6df873688010" ts="2026-11-06T01:55:03.108005+00:00" -->

**Input**

```json
{"command":"cd ~/.xgd/worktrees/git_github.com_lagrangefoundry_1stcontact.git/free-BUG-359 && sed -n 1,160p tests/test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_deploy_prunes_old_registry_images.test.ts","description":"Read the registry-prune UAT"}
```

**Result**

```
/**
 * [[BUG-359]] fix 3 — A PRODUCTION DEPLOY PRUNES THE APP'S OLDER JOB IMAGES.
 *
 * Every production deploy pushes a ~1.5 GB job image to the account's Cloudflare
 * registry and nothing deleted the old ones. After a successful cloud deploy,
 * `bin/deploy` runs `1c images prune`, which keeps the image the app's container
 * application now runs plus the one before it (a one-version rollback) and
 * deletes the rest. A delete that fails warns; the deploy has already shipped.
 *
 * The registry and the Cloudflare API are stubbed at the HTTP boundary
 * (`fetch`): the account's container application, the Worker's versions, the
 * registry's credentials, its catalog, and its manifests. The app, its Worker
 * name and its container class are read from the real `wrangler.toml`.
 */
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import path from 'node:path'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { containerClasses, pruneRegistryImages } from '../tools/generate/src/cli/container-images'

const REPO = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..')
const CONTROL_APP = path.join(REPO, 'apps', 'control-app')
const ACCOUNT = 'acct-bug359'
const REGISTRY = 'registry.cloudflare.com'

/** The production Worker's name, as `wrangler.toml` gives it. */
function productionWorker(): string {
  const toml = readFileSync(path.join(CONTROL_APP, 'wrangler.toml'), 'utf8')
  const block = toml.slice(toml.indexOf('[env.production]'))
  return /^name\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/m.exec(block)![1]!
}

/** A Cloudflare account and registry holding `tags` for one container application. */
function cloudflare(opts: { application: string; repository: string; deployed: string; tags: string[]; refuse?: string[] }) {
  const tags = [...opts.tags]
  const deletes: string[] = []
  const uploaded = (tag: string) => `2026-11-0${opts.tags.indexOf(tag) + 1}T12:00:00Z`
  const fetch = (async (input: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const url = String(input)
    const method = init?.method ?? 'GET'
    const ok = (result: unknown) => Response.json({ success: true, result })
    if (url.endsWith(`/accounts/${ACCOUNT}/containers/applications`)) {
      return ok([{ name: opts.application, configuration: { image: `${REGISTRY}/${ACCOUNT}/${opts.repository}:${opts.deployed}` } }])
    }
    if (url.includes(`/accounts/${ACCOUNT}/workers/scripts/`) && url.includes('/versions')) {
      return ok({ items: opts.tags.map((t) => ({ id: `${t}-0000-0000-0000-000000000000`, metadata: { created_on: uploaded(t) } })) })
    }
    if (url.endsWith(`/containers/registries/${REGISTRY}/credentials`) && method === 'POST') return ok({ password: 'registry-password' })
    if (url === `https://${REGISTRY}/v2/_catalog?tags=true`) return Response.json({ repositories: { [`${ACCOUNT}/${opts.repository}`]: [...tags] } })
    const manifest = new RegExp(`^https://${REGISTRY}/v2/${ACCOUNT}/${opts.repository}/manifests/(.+)$`).exec(url)
    if (manifest) {
      const tag = manifest[1]!
      if (!tags.includes(tag)) return new Response(null, { status: 404 })
      if (method === 'DELETE') {
        if (opts.refuse?.includes(tag)) return new Response(null, { status: 500 })
        deletes.push(tag)
        tags.splice(tags.indexOf(tag), 1)
      }
      return new Response(null, { status: 200 })
    }
    if (url === `https://${REGISTRY}/v2/gc/layers` && method === 'PUT') return new Response(null, { status: 202 })
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: false, errors: [{ message: `unexpected ${method} ${url}` }] }), { status: 404 })
  }) as typeof globalThis.fetch
  return { fetch, tags, deletes }
}

describe('BUG-359 — a production deploy prunes old registry images', () => {
  const worker = productionWorker()
  const classes = containerClasses(CONTROL_APP)
  const application = `${worker}-${classes[0]!.toLowerCase()}`

  it('test_UAT_FC_BUG-359_deploy_prunes_old_registry_images', async () => {
    expect(classes).toEqual(['AgentContainer'])
```

## Bash cd ~/.xgd/worktrees/git_github.com_lagrangefoundry_1stcontact.git/free-BUG-359 && sed -n 100,260p tests/reconciliation-p…
<!-- xgd-tool name="Bash" id="toolu_01AA9WR1aGiPDEnSLJPH2o1c" turn="78b736464f964ade910a6df873688010" ts="2026-11-06T01:55:10.930129+00:00" -->

**Input**

```json
{"command":"cd ~/.xgd/worktrees/git_github.com_lagrangefoundry_1stcontact.git/free-BUG-359 && sed -n 100,260p tests/reconciliation-platform-build-deploy-smoke.test.ts; sed -n 625,655p bin/deploy","description":"Read smoke suite makeTree and the deploy prune block"}
```

**Result**

```
interface Run {
  code: number
  out: string
  err: string
  all: string
}

function sh(cmd: string, args: string[], opts: { cwd: string; env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv }): Run {
  const res = spawnSync(cmd, args, {
    cwd: opts.cwd,
    encoding: 'utf8',
    env: { ...process.env, ...opts.env },
  })
  const out = res.stdout ?? ''
  const err = res.stderr ?? ''
  return { code: res.status ?? -1, out, err, all: out + err }
}

// ── fixture tree: the real scripts, a tree we control, recording shims ───────

const roots: string[] = []
afterEach(() => {
  for (const root of roots.splice(0)) rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
})

interface HookSpec {
  kind: 'migrate' | 'secrets'
  name: string
  /** Bash body, minus the shebang. Defaults to recording the deploy context. */
  body?: string
  /** false writes it 0644 — documentation, which must never run. */
  executable?: boolean
}

interface Tree {
  root: string
  /** Everything the shims and hooks recorded, in order. */
  log: () => string[]
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv
}

/** Record the deploy context a hook was handed, as one parseable line. */
function contextHook(label: string): string {
  const fields = 'hook|%s|app=%s|dir=%s|env=%s|name=%s|dry=%s|root=%s'
  const values = '"$DEPLOY_APP" "$DEPLOY_APP_DIR" "$DEPLOY_ENV" "$DEPLOY_WORKER_NAME" "$DEPLOY_DRY_RUN" "$DEPLOY_REPO_ROOT"'
  return `printf '${fields}\\n' '${label}' ${values} >> "$SHIM_LOG"\n`
}

function writeScript(file: string, body: string, executable = true): void {
  writeFileSync(file, `#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n${body}`)
  chmodSync(file, executable ? 0o755 : 0o644)
}

function makeTree(opts: {
  label: string
  apps?: string[]
  hooks?: HookSpec[]
  /** Exit code the fixture tree's `bin/1c` stub returns. */
  oneCExit?: number
}): Tree {
  const root = realpathSync(mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), `d5167ced-${opts.label}-`)))
  roots.push(root)

  // The real scripts, byte-for-byte — they resolve their repo root from here.
  mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'bin'), { recursive: true })
  for (const script of ['build', 'deploy']) {
    const dest = path.join(root, 'bin', script)
    copyFileSync(path.join(REPO, 'bin', script), dest)
    chmodSync(dest, 0o755)
  }
  // `bin/1c` is a separate command with its own criterion (AC-1330); here it is
  // a stub so `bin/build`'s ORDERING and exit-code propagation are observable.
  writeScript(
    path.join(root, 'bin', '1c'),
    `printf '1c|%s\\n' "$*" >> "$SHIM_LOG"\necho "stub preflight"\nexit "\${ONE_C_EXIT:-0}"\n`,
  )

  // THE SHARED HOOK MACHINERY TRAVELS WITH THE DRIVER ([[REQ-264]]). `bin/deploy`
  // prints the capability report by running `bin/deploy.d/lib/probe.mjs`, and the
  // hooks probe through `secret.sh` — a fixture tree holding the driver without
  // them would be a tree the real driver cannot run in, which is the one thing
  // this fixture exists not to be.
  mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'bin', 'deploy.d', 'lib'), { recursive: true })
  for (const file of ['probe.mjs', 'secret.sh']) {
    copyFileSync(
      path.join(REPO, 'bin', 'deploy.d', 'lib', file),
      path.join(root, 'bin', 'deploy.d', 'lib', file),
    )
  }

  // AND SO DOES THE SECRETS-FILE LOADER ([[REQ-393]]): both drivers re-run
  // themselves through it before anything else, so without it neither starts.
  mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'tools', 'generate', 'bin'), { recursive: true })
  copyFileSync(
    path.join(REPO, 'tools', 'generate', 'bin', 'env-file.mjs'),
    path.join(root, 'tools', 'generate', 'bin', 'env-file.mjs'),
  )

  for (const kind of ['migrate', 'secrets'] as const) {
    const dir = path.join(root, 'bin', 'deploy.d', kind)
    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true })
    // Documentation beside the hooks — non-executable, so never run.
    writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'README.md'), `# ${kind} hooks (fixture)\n`)
    chmodSync(path.join(dir, 'README.md'), 0o644)
  }
  for (const hook of opts.hooks ?? []) {
    writeScript(
      path.join(root, 'bin', 'deploy.d', hook.kind, hook.name),
      hook.body ?? contextHook(`${hook.kind}/${hook.name}`),
      hook.executable !== false,
    )
  }

  mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apps'), { recursive: true })
  for (const app of opts.apps ?? []) {
    mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apps', app, 'src'), { recursive: true })
    writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apps', app, 'src', 'index.ts'), 'export default {}\n')
    writeFileSync(
      path.join(root, 'apps', app, 'wrangler.toml'),
      // The deployed Worker's name deliberately differs from the directory, so
      // a hook reading DEPLOY_WORKER_NAME cannot pass by accident.
      `name = "worker-${app}"\nmain = "src/index.ts"\n\n` +
        `[env.production]\nname = "prod-${app}"\n\n` +
        `[env.staging]\nname = "stage-${app}"\n`,
    )
  }

  // pnpm and wrangler are the external boundary: recorded, never run.
  const shim = path.join(root, 'shim')
  mkdirSync(shim, { recursive: true })
  writeScript(path.join(shim, 'pnpm'), `printf 'pnpm|%s|%s\\n' "$*" "$PWD" >> "$SHIM_LOG"\n`)
  writeScript(
    path.join(shim, 'npx'),
    `printf 'npx|%s|%s\\n' "$*" "$PWD" >> "$SHIM_LOG"\n` +
      // Real wrangler writes the bundle; `bin/build` reports what it finds.
      `if [[ "$*" == *"--outdir dist"* ]]; then mkdir -p dist && printf x > dist/index.js; fi\n`,
  )

  const shimLog = path.join(root, 'shim.log')
  writeFileSync(shimLog, '')

  return {
    root,
    log: () =>
      readFileSync(shimLog, 'utf8')
        .split('\n')
        .filter((l) => l !== ''),
    env: {
      PATH: `${shim}:${process.env.PATH ?? ''}`,
      SHIM_LOG: shimLog,
      ONE_C_EXIT: String(opts.oneCExit ?? 0),
    },
  }
}

/** PATH shims for a run against the REAL repo — discovery must see the real tree. */
function realRepoShims(la
```

```
  it('test_UAT_AC1332_rehearsal_runs_the_same_hooks_and_composes_the_same_invocation', () => {
    const hooks: HookSpec[] = [
      { kind: 'migrate', name: '10-migrate' },
      { kind: 'secrets', name: '10-secret' },
    ]

    const rehearsal = makeTree({ label: 'rehearse', apps: ['alpha'], hooks })
    const dry = sh(path.join(rehearsal.root, 'bin', 'deploy'), ['--dry-run', 'alpha'], {
      cwd: rehearsal.root,
      env: rehearsal.env,
    })
    expect(dry.code, dry.all).toBe(0)

    const genuine = makeTree({ label: 'deploy', apps: ['alpha'], hooks })
    const wet = sh(path.join(genuine.root, 'bin', 'deploy'), ['alpha'], {
      cwd: genuine.root,
      env: genuine.env,
    })
    expect(wet.code, wet.all).toBe(0)

    // anything — a real deploy says neither.
    expect(dry.out).toContain('DRY RUN — hooks are told not to change anything, and nothing is uploaded.')
```

**Result**

```
---
name: job-container-uat-rig
description: "Running REQ-646's container UATs in a worktree: dist-assets symlink, fresh src/generated after merging, one test per call."
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: reference
  originSessionId: 0937d360-88d6-4d5c-af48-d851451d5716
  modified: 2026-11-05T00:26:18.214Z
---

`tests/test_UAT_FC_REQ-646_agent_compute_in_containers.test.ts` runs the control app under `unstable_dev` with real job containers (Docker).
- **A worktree needs `apps/control-app/dist-assets`**, or `unstable_dev` refuses the config. Symlink the main checkout's copy, and never stage the symlink.
- **Re-copy `apps/control-app/src/generated` after merging xgd-working into a branch.** The stale copy made BUG-322's stage-guide UAT fail on the branch only, which looked like a regression.
- **Run one case per Bash call** (`-t <name>`): each takes 1–8 min (the 400-product/2,000-item case is about 500 s), so the whole file overruns 590 s.
- `getPlatformProxy` on the full `wrangler.toml` fails because the AI binding is `remote = true`. The rig seeds through a D1/R2-only config whose persist path is `<persistTo>/v3`.

Related: [[docker-builds-need-temp-docker-config]], [[worktree-and-store-setup]], [[full-suite-in-foreground-batches]].
total 0
drwxrwxr-x   5 martin  staff   160 Nov  5 17:58 .
drwxrwxr-x  13 martin  staff   416 Nov  5 17:58 ..
```
